# Push / Pull / Legs
_Your Blaze Camp plan_

- **Main goal:** Build muscle
- Muscle & physique
- **Level:** Advanced (L3): 4 years of consistent training, training 6 days a week now
- **Plan type:** 12 weeks (recommended) · Week 1-12
- **Start date:** 2026-10-12
- **Training days per week:** 6 · Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Saturday · Session length (minutes): 75
- Release accepted (Liability release (client), English)

## Before you start

- Your schedule cannot fit 10 weekly sets for Side delts, Calves: make sessions about 85 min
- Starting sets were lowered for Quads, Hamstrings, Glutes, Chest, Back (lats / upper back), Side delts, Biceps, Triceps, Calves so the stairway fits your 75-minute sessions.

**Your schedule options**
- Keep 75 minutes: lower starting sets, same stairway (this plan)

## Basic Instruction
> \*Always leave 1 rep in the tank, never work up to failure but get as close as you can.
>
> \*\*Do steady cardio whenever you want, just don't overdo it... I recommend doing it on your aerobic days, max 40 min of steady.
>
> \*\*\*The lower the reps, the higher the weight... Always go for 1-3 RIR (reps in reserve), aka RPE 7+.
>
> \*\*\*\*When there are joint pains, usually going unilateral and/or getting away from fixed grips (barbells, bars) and going to split grips (dumbbells, kettlebells, individual grips) helps take care of some stuff.
>
> \*\*\*\*\*If you have any questions just ask me - IG @BLAZECAMP or TWITTER @BLAZECAMP\_

Planned effort across the stairway: weeks 1-6: leave 3 in the tank; weeks 7-9: leave 2 in the tank; weeks 10-11: leave 1-2 in the tank; week 12: leave 1 in the tank. The last week may add one intensifier on a small-muscle exercise.

How heavy? Each exercise tells you how many reps to leave in the tank: "leave 2 in the tank" means you could do 2 more reps at the end of the set (about RPE 8; 3 left = RPE 7, 1 left = RPE 9). The first weeks leave more in the tank, the last weeks less. Week 1: start a little lighter, with a weight you could lift 2-3 more times.

Your plan climbs like a stairway: small steps first, a bit bigger after the halfway week, the hardest weeks at the end.

## Week 1 — Getting used to it (small steps)

_Effort level this week: 2 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**Baseline test this week (before you load up)** (Strength)

### Monday · Push
_about 64 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **KB Book Opener**
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=KB%20Book%20Opener)

**Core**
1. **Crunch** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 30-60 s rest
   - Optional: add a core superset to any exercise
   - Swap for: Plank (forearm / low plank) / Sit-Up / Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Crunch)
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 20-60 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) / Reverse Crunch
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aDsaGBnvDQo)

**Strength**
1. **Incline Barbell Bench Press** — 3 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Test: work up to one top set of 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max (warm-up sets first), then record the weight.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.
   - Swap for: Barbell Bench Press / DB Floor Press / Incline DB Bench Press / Flat DB Bench Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Incline%20Barbell%20Bench%20Press)
2. **Alternating Flat DB Bench Press** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) / Alternating DB Floor Press / Alternating Standing Cable Press (one forward / one back) / Incline DB Bench Press
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YUhAJt8xsFY)
3. **Alternating DB Overhead Press** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down) / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press / Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20DB%20Overhead%20Press)
4. **Weighted Dip** — 3 × 5-8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - Reps capped for this exercise (5-8): it is harder than its group.
   > Counts 0.5 chest + 0.5 triceps ('more tricep than chest but still .5 and .5'); program it on a push day as a push exercise.
   - Swap for: Parallel-Bar Dip / Assisted Dips (machine) / Incline Barbell Bench Press / Barbell Bench Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Dip)

**Accessories**
1. **Flat DB Fly** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Cable Fly / Machine Fly (Pec Deck)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Fly)
2. **EZ-Bar Skullcrusher** — 2 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Reps capped for this exercise (8-12): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Tate Press / Floor Dumbbell Skullcrusher / Overhead Cable Triceps Extension / Cable Triceps Push-down
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=EZ-Bar%20Skullcrusher)
3. **Seated DB Lateral Raise** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Incline / Side-Lying Cable Lateral Raise / Cable Side Raise (leaning) / DB Upright Row / DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20DB%20Lateral%20Raise)

**Aerobic**
1. **Aerobic C (orange): 11 Minute Run/ 2 Min Walk** — Orange (C): long / easy · 30-40 min
   - Any cardio tool works: run, bike, rower or ski erg. Track time, calories or distance.
   - Separate session: any time of day (not added to your lifting time).
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20C%20(orange)%3A%2011%20Minute%20Run%2F%202%20Min%20Walk)

**Cool-down**
1. Long walk after your workout: 20-30 min easy

   - Swap for: Cable Curl (Zone 1 / Zone 2 / Zone 3 set-ups) / Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) / Reverse-Grip (Pronated) EZ-Bar Curl / EZ-Bar Curl

## Week 2 — Getting used to it (small steps)

_Effort level this week: 2.6 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Seated Dumbbell Curl (Tuesday)
- Biceps is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Calves is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

- +1 set on Single-Arm DB/KB Row (Tuesday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

   - Swap for: Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down) / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press / Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20DB%20Overhead%20Press)
4. **Weighted Dip** — 3 × 5-8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Reps capped for this exercise (5-8): it is harder than its group.
   > Counts 0.5 chest + 0.5 triceps ('more tricep than chest but still .5 and .5'); program it on a push day as a push exercise.
   - Swap for: Parallel-Bar Dip / Assisted Dips (machine) / Incline Barbell Bench Press / Barbell Bench Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Dip)

**Accessories**

   - Reps capped for this exercise (8-12): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Tate Press / Floor Dumbbell Skullcrusher / Overhead Cable Triceps Extension / Cable Triceps Push-down
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=EZ-Bar%20Skullcrusher)

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20DB%20Lateral%20Raise)

**Aerobic**

   - Second calf exercise: 1 set(s) (3 calf sets today in total)

1. Long walk after your workout: 20-30 min easy

## Week 5 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 4.4 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- New exercise block: one small change on some exercises
- +1 set on Incline Barbell Bench Press (Monday)
- Triceps is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Russian%20Twist)
2. **Knees to Chest (hanging)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Leg Raise (lying / supine) / Reverse Crunch / Copenhagen Plank / Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Knees%20to%20Chest%20(hanging))

**Strength**
1. **Stiff-Leg Deadlift / RDL** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 3-6 min rest
   - Swap for: Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift / Sumo Deadlift / Wide-Grip Barbell Deadlift / Barbell Deadlift (conventional)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Stiff-Leg%20Deadlift%20%2F%20RDL)
2. **Leg Press (machine)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   > Constrained, no axial (spinal) load: the default second squat-pattern lift of the week (PER-04).
   - Swap for: Landmine Hack Squat / Low DB Squat (two DBs hanging at the sides) / Sumo Squat (DB or KB) / Landmine Squat
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Press%20(machine))
3. **Reverse Lunge** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Step-Up (forward and lateral) / Walking Lunge / Side (Lateral) Lunge / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/7pwO2gemRyg)
4. **Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift / Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg Glute Bridge (foot elevated on bench)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rear-Foot-on-Wall%20DB%20Deadlift%20(RDL))

**Accessories**
1. **Sissy Squat - different stance** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Leg Extension (machine) / DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/HW5yUsjKru4)
2. **Machine Leg Curl (seated or lying)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Lying Leg Curl (machine) / Nordic Hamstring Curl / Seated Leg Curl (machine) / Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Leg%20Curl%20(seated%20or%20lying))
3. **Machine Calf Raise** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise)
4. **Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)** — 1 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Second calf exercise: 1 set(s) (3 calf sets today in total)
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Machine Calf Raise / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20Calf%20Raise%20(bodyweight%2C%20from%20a%20deficit%2Fstep))
5. **Reverse Hyperextension (Focus)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Extra exercise to reach your weekly sets
   > Use if available and the client feels the right muscles.
   - Swap for: Hip Thrust (machine) / Feet-Elevated Glute Bridge (feet on bench) / Barbell Hip Thrust / Single-Leg Glute Bridge (foot elevated on bench)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/3vmbvoT2m-U)

**Cool-down**
1. Long walk after your workout: 20-30 min easy

## Week 6 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 5 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Seated DB Lateral Raise (Monday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

### Monday · Push
_about 68 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **KB Book Opener**
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=KB%20Book%20Opener)

**Core**
1. **Crunch** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Plank (forearm / low plank) / Sit-Up / Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Crunch)
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 20-60 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) / Reverse Crunch
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aDsaGBnvDQo)

**Strength**
1. **Incline Barbell Bench Press** — 4 × 12/10/8 -20 · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Bench Press / DB Floor Press / Incline DB Bench Press / Flat DB Bench Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Incline%20Barbell%20Bench%20Press)
2. **Alternating Flat DB Bench Press** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) / Alternating DB Floor Press / Alternating Standing Cable Press (one forward / one back) / Incline DB Bench Press

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20DB%20Overhead%20Press)
4. **Weighted Dip - different implement** — 3 × 5-8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Reps capped for this exercise (5-8): it is harder than its group.
   > Counts 0.5 chest + 0.5 triceps ('more tricep than chest but still .5 and .5'); program it on a push day as a push exercise.
   - Swap for: Parallel-Bar Dip / Assisted Dips (machine) / Incline Barbell Bench Press / Barbell Bench Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Dip%20-%20different%20implement)

**Accessories**

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20DB%20Lateral%20Raise)

**Aerobic**

   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Incline-Bench Prone DB T-Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
2. **Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck)** — 2 × 20 to 30 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Face Pull / Upward Face Pull (low-to-high / overhead finish)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Reverse%20Fly%20(rear-delt%20pec%20deck))
3. **Seated Dumbbell Curl** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) / Incline Dumbbell Curl ('pinky facing up' supinated variation) / Dumbbell Concentration Curl / Supine (Lying) Biceps Curl
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20Dumbbell%20Curl)
4. **Cable Curl (Zone 1 / Zone 2 / Zone 3 set-ups)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   > Zone 1 = arms down by the sides (vertical, external-rotation bias); zone 2 = arms in front at shoulder height (horizontal, internal-rotation bias, compressive); zone 3 = arms overhead (vertical, external-rotation bias).
   - Swap for: Dumbbell Concentration Curl / Supine (Lying) Biceps Curl / EZ-Bar Curl / Cross-Body Dumbbell Hammer Curl
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Curl%20(Zone%201%20%2F%20Zone%202%20%2F%20Zone%203%20set-ups))

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20C%20(orange)%3A%202%20Mile%20Run)

   > Zone 1 = arms down by the sides (vertical, external-rotation bias); zone 2 = arms in front at shoulder height (horizontal, internal-rotation bias, compressive); zone 3 = arms overhead (vertical, external-rotation bias).

   - Swap for: Crunch / Plank (forearm / low plank) / Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / Side Plank (elbow)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Sit-Up)
2. **Toes to Bar (hanging)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Copenhagen Plank / Reverse Crunch / Knees to Chest (hanging) / Leg Raise (lying / supine)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Toes%20to%20Bar%20(hanging))

**Strength**
1. **Barbell Bench Press** — 4 × 12/10/8 -20 · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press / Flat DB Bench Press / DB Floor Press / Incline DB Bench Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Bench%20Press)
2. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline DB Bench Press / Flat DB Bench Press / DB Floor Press / Machine Bench Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press%20(one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down))
3. **Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict) / High-Incline Seated Barbell Press / High-Incline DB Press / Standing DB Overhead Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seesaw%20DB%20Press%20(alternating%2C%20one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down))
4. **Weighted Dip** — 2 × 5-8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest
   - Reps capped for this exercise (5-8): it is harder than its group.
   > Counts 0.5 chest + 0.5 triceps ('more tricep than chest but still .5 and .5'); program it on a push day as a push exercise.
   - Swap for: Parallel-Bar Dip / Assisted Dips (machine) / Barbell Bench Press / Incline Barbell Bench Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Dip)

**Accessories**
1. **Cable Fly** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Flat DB Fly / Machine Fly (Pec Deck)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Fly)
2. **Tate Press** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: EZ-Bar Skullcrusher / Floor Dumbbell Skullcrusher / Cable Triceps Push-down / Cable Triceps Pushdown - straight bar, pronated grip
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Tate%20Press)
3. **Cable Side Raise (leaning)** — 2 × 15 to 20 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · last set MAX (technical failure) · 60-90 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline / Side-Lying Cable Lateral Raise / Seated DB Lateral Raise / DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip) / DB Upright Row
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=lq7eLC30b9w)

**Aerobic**
1. **Aerobic B (green): 2 Minute run/ 4 Min Walk** — Green (B): moderate · 20-30 min
   - Any cardio tool works: run, bike, rower or ski erg. Track time, calories or distance.
   - Separate session: any time of day (not added to your lifting time).
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20B%20(green)%3A%202%20Minute%20run%2F%204%20Min%20Walk)

**Cool-down**
1. Long walk after your workout: 20-30 min easy

## Week 9 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 8 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- New exercise block: one small change on some exercises
- +1 set on Incline Barbell Bench Press (Monday)
- +1 set on Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) (Friday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

4. **Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 60-120 s rest

- +1 set on Barbell Bench Press (Thursday)

## Week 11 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 10 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Reverse Hyperextension (Saturday)
- Glutes is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

_about 70 min_

   - Swap for: Machine Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Barbell Calf Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20Calf%20Raise%20(bodyweight%2C%20from%20a%20deficit%2Fstep))
5. **Reverse Hyperextension (Focus)** — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Extra exercise to reach your weekly sets
   > Use if available and the client feels the right muscles.
   - Swap for: Hip Thrust (machine) / Feet-Elevated Glute Bridge (feet on bench) / Barbell Hip Thrust / Single-Leg Glute Bridge (foot elevated on bench)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/3vmbvoT2m-U)

**Cool-down**
1. Long walk after your workout: 20-30 min easy

## Week 12 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 11 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**End test this week: replaces this week's top sets** (Strength)

_last week: intensifiers allowed (MYO set on the last machine / isolation exercise)_

**What changes this week**
- New bonus exercise: Machine Fly (Pec Deck) (Monday)
- +1 set on Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) (Friday)
- +1 set on High-Bar Back Squat (Wednesday)
- +1 set on Stiff-Leg Deadlift / RDL (Saturday)
- +1 set on Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly (Friday)
- Quads is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Hamstrings is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Chest is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Back (lats / upper back) is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Curl%20(Zone%201%20%2F%20Zone%202%20%2F%20Zone%203%20set-ups))

**Aerobic**
1. **Aerobic C (orange): 2 Mile Run** — Orange (C): long / easy · 30-40 min
   - Any cardio tool works: run, bike, rower or ski erg. Track time, calories or distance.
   - Separate session: any time of day (not added to your lifting time).
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20C%20(orange)%3A%202%20Mile%20Run)

**Cool-down**
1. Long walk after your workout: 20-30 min easy

### Saturday · Legs 2 - hinge-led (leg press)
_about 73 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **Medball Reverse Lunge with Medball Up**
   - Swap for: Medball RFE Split Squat (Bulgarian) with Medball Up / Medball Lateral Cossack Lunge with Overhead Reach / Medball Reverse Lunge with Cross-Body Turn
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Reverse%20Lunge%20with%20Medball%20Up)

**Core**
1. **Russian Twist** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / Crunch / Sit-Up / Plank (forearm / low plank)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Russian%20Twist)
2. **Knees to Chest (hanging)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Leg Raise (lying / supine) / Reverse Crunch / Copenhagen Plank / Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Knees%20to%20Chest%20(hanging))

**Strength**
1. **Stiff-Leg Deadlift / RDL** — 4 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Test: work up to one top set of 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max (warm-up sets first), then record the weight.
   - Test day: replaces this week's top sets
   - Swap for: Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift / Sumo Deadlift / Wide-Grip Barbell Deadlift / Barbell Deadlift (conventional)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Stiff-Leg%20Deadlift%20%2F%20RDL)
2. **Hack Squat (machine)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   > No ranking: 'if the hack squat that you have in your gym works and you feel it is good, then that's great.' More constrained than a Smith machine.
   - Swap for: Leg Press (machine) / Landmine Hack Squat / Low DB Squat (two DBs hanging at the sides) / Sumo Squat (DB or KB)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hack%20Squat%20(machine))
3. **Reverse Lunge** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Step-Up (forward and lateral) / Walking Lunge / Side (Lateral) Lunge / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/7pwO2gemRyg)
4. **Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift / Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg Glute Bridge (foot elevated on bench)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rear-Foot-on-Wall%20DB%20Deadlift%20(RDL))

**Accessories**
1. **Sissy Squat** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Leg Extension (machine) / DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/HW5yUsjKru4)
2. **Machine Leg Curl (seated or lying)** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Lying Leg Curl (machine) / Nordic Hamstring Curl / Seated Leg Curl (machine) / Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Leg%20Curl%20(seated%20or%20lying))
3. **Barbell Calf Raise** — 2 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Machine Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Calf%20Raise)
4. **Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)** — 1 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · last set MAX (technical failure) · 60-90 s rest
   - Second calf exercise: 1 set(s) (3 calf sets today in total)
   - Swap for: Machine Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Barbell Calf Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20Calf%20Raise%20(bodyweight%2C%20from%20a%20deficit%2Fstep))
5. **Reverse Hyperextension (Focus)** — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set · 60-90 s rest
   - Extra exercise to reach your weekly sets
   > Use if available and the client feels the right muscles.
   - Swap for: Hip Thrust (machine) / Feet-Elevated Glute Bridge (feet on bench) / Barbell Hip Thrust / Single-Leg Glute Bridge (foot elevated on bench)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/3vmbvoT2m-U)

**Cool-down**
1. Long walk after your workout: 20-30 min easy

## Notes

- ⚠ Two-leg / single-leg share follows the plan (V28, 1)
